32:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["Recursive array aliases retain an array definition with ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"items"}]," in ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"components.schemas"}],"; recursive edges point back to that definition or the surrounding value union. For example, ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"type JsonArray = JsonValue[]"}]," in a recursive JSON value type produces an array component rather than a component containing only a reference to itself. Different length constraints on uses of the same recursive array get separate components, so a ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"tags.MaxItems<1>"}]," use does not constrain an untagged sibling. 3a:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["For Swagger 2 documents, ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"OpenApiConverter.upgradeDocument"}]," composes each server URL from ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"schemes"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"host"}],", and ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"basePath"}]," and rejects endpoint fields containing queries or fragments. 3b:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":[["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"OpenApiConverter.downgradeDocument(document, \"2.0\")"}]," keeps media types on each operation instead of guessing shared document defaults.
